# Full keyword report — 2 November 2026

Research only. Nothing on the live site has changed. This is the full map for you to confirm before anything is built.

## Summary

| Group | Items | Primary keyword | Secondary keywords | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Products | 212 | 212 (16 have no Semrush data, see below) | 1,146 (5–10 each) | all at 5 or more |
| Blog posts | 84 | 84 | 858 (10–15 each) | all at 10 or more |
| Pages | 11 | 11 (About, Contact, FAQ and Blog are new) | 128 (10–15 each) | all at 10 or more |
| Categories | 10 | 10 | 76 (5–10 each) | all at 5 or more |
| **Total** | **317** | **317** | **2,208 placements, 1,278 different keywords** | **none without or short** |

Every secondary keyword is an exact phrase from your Semrush files, Transactional or Commercial, KD 29 or lower. The exceptions are the Informational top-ups on blog posts, which stay at 30% or less of each post, and 12 Informational cut-name keywords kept from earlier rounds on 8 products (wagyu porterhouse, oyster blade, sliced brisket, wagyu sausages, beef brisket cut and similar).

## What was done this round

1. **Loaded the 19 new files** (schnitzel, drumstick, Maryland, hanger steak, tri tip, oxtail and tendon). The 2026-11-01 files were also re-read.
2. **Fixed two data bugs:**
   - **Tri-tip file:** it has an extra "Trend" column, so the difficulty was being read from the wrong column. Tri tip now shows its real KD.
   - **Exclusion filter:** it matched parts of words, so "rice" was blocking every keyword containing "price", and "gravy" was blocking "gravy beef". It now matches whole words only, which freed many "price" keywords for products.
3. **Raised the difficulty limit to KD 29**, as you asked.
4. **Gave every item a verified primary:**
   - Beef Liver, Beef Tongue, Beef Tendon and Oxtail had no primary in the plan. They now use beef liver meat, beef tongue (KD 29), beef tendon and oxtail.
   - Eleven planned primaries had no Semrush data in any file, so I replaced them with exact file keywords: beef rump cap, rump cap beef, beef spider steak, gravy beef, beef shin hammer, lamb backstrap, wagyu topside, beef karubi, lamb loin, lamb sausages and chicken drumettes.
   - About, Contact, FAQ and Blog had placeholder primaries. They now use halal butcher, halal butcher near me, check halal food and beef meals.
5. **Filled every short item.** I searched all CSV files for Transactional and Commercial keywords at KD 29 or lower, in this order:
   1. Keywords for the exact cut.
   2. Species-level halal and buying keywords for the same meat (for example "halal lamb meat near me" on a lamb product).
   3. Your original October keyword files, only where the refreshed files had nothing. These are marked ⓞ.
6. **Removed misfits during the run.** These included "organic beef sausages" matching Beef Tongue (because "organ" matched inside "organic"), other cuts' prices on Spider Steaks, cat-food and foie-gras keywords on the pâté post, "non halal" and "goat head" keywords, Boer or baby goat claims, people's names (Rick Stein, Nigella, Ottolenghi), restaurants, US-only searches and pet food.

## How to read each entry

- **URL with the primary keyword:** the slug must contain the primary (your rule). Where the current URL doesn't, a new URL is given and the old one 301s to it.
- **SEO title:** starts with the primary keyword, has 1–2 power words from the approved list, and is 60 characters or less.
- **Meta description:** 110–158 characters and contains the primary plus the two most natural secondaries.
- **Image alt:** contains the primary. The image title, caption and JSON-LD ImageObject use the same text, per the image-SEO rule.
- **Times used:** the planned number of places each keyword appears once built, with the places listed. Products use the primary 12 times, categories 9, posts 11 and pages 10 (the homepage and blog index 9, as they have no keyword URL). Each secondary appears 3–5 times: in the description or body, an H2/H3 (for the first few), tags, an FAQ question, and the meta description (for two of them).
- **Marks:**
  - \* means a keyword that is also used on another page (your reuse rule). In blog posts, a reused keyword links to its home page; the link is shown in the "Where" column.
  - ⓞ means the data comes from your October 12–13 files, because the keyword wasn't in the refreshed files.
- **Intent / Volume / KD:** taken from the newest file that has the keyword.

## Decisions needed before the build

1. **243 URL changes.**
   - The new primaries mostly don't appear in the current URLs, so 243 URLs change. All of them will get a 301 from the old URL.
   - Changing this many URLs at once resets some ranking history for a few weeks, even with redirects.
   - If you would rather keep current URLs where possible, the alternative is to relax the "keyword in slug" rule for products whose current slug already names the product.
2. **Blog index URL.** It stays /blog/, because every post sits under it; the keyword goes in the title, H1 and meta. Please confirm this exception.
3. **About, Contact and FAQ URLs.** These would move to /about-halal-butcher/, /contact-halal-butcher-near-me/ and /faq-check-halal-food/.
4. **16 products whose primary has no Semrush data in any file.** These are your plan keywords or the product name. To verify or replace them, please search these terms in Semrush:

   | Product | Current primary | Search in Semrush |
   |---|---|---|
   | Wagyu Blade Steaks MB5+ | blade steak | wagyu blade steak, blade steak |
   | Beef Mince with 15% Liver | beef liver mince | beef and liver mince, organ meat mince |
   | Lamb Rump | buy lamb rump steak online | lamb rump |
   | Lamb Full Plate Ribs | lamb flap cut | lamb ribs, lamb flaps |
   | Lamb Ham | lamb ham australia | lamb ham |
   | Chimichurri Marinated Chicken Wings | product name | chimichurri chicken, marinated chicken wings |
   | Buffalo Marinated Chicken Wings | product name | buffalo wings, buffalo chicken wings |
   | Portuguese Marinated Split Chicken | piri piri chicken near me | piri piri chicken, portuguese chicken |
   | Smokey Heat Marinated Split Chicken | smoky chicken near me | smoked chicken, bbq chicken |
   | Truffle Buffalo Marinated Split Chicken | buffalo chicken | buffalo chicken, truffle chicken |
   | Duck Bones Carton | duck bone | duck bones, duck carcass |
   | Beef Rump Cryovac Carton | bulk rump steak | rump steak bulk, whole rump |
   | Lamb Leg Cryovac Carton | whole lamb leg price | lamb leg bulk, whole lamb leg |
   | Lamb Curry Pieces Carton | bulk meat packs | lamb curry meat, curry lamb |
   | Goat Curry Cut Catering Pack | goat curry near me | goat curry meat, goat curry cut |
   | Chicken Curry Cut Catering Pack | chicken curry near me | chicken curry cut, curry chicken |

5. **25 primaries now have a refreshed KD above 29.** You said to keep primaries as they are, so they are unchanged. The highest are: roast beef 63, beef short ribs 62, beef roast 58, mongolian beef 55, kofta 53, hanger steak 50 and beef steak ribeye 48. The full list is in each entry.
6. **Product claims now used in keywords**, which you confirmed:
   - grass fed: 16 placements
   - lean: 27
   - organic: 11
   - cheap: 15
   - free range: 4

   CLAUDE.md and `COMPLIANCE.bannedTerms` must be updated at build time, otherwise the build check fails. Please keep evidence on file for organic and free-range claims (ACCC).
7. **"Frozen" is still on hold.** The chimichurri wings primary was "frozen chicken wings", so it is now the product name until you tell me which products are frozen.
8. **Keyword reuse.**
   - A keyword appears on at most 6 product or page entries, and at most 10 entries where it is a generic species keyword (e.g. "halal lamb") or used in blog posts.
   - This conflicts with the build check that allows one keyword per page (F15), so F15 has to be relaxed at build time.
   - The risk is pages competing for the same search. To limit it, a reused keyword is never another page's primary, and in posts it always links back to its home.
9. **Legal pages (Refund, Terms, Privacy)** have no keyword target. Legal pages aren't search targets; I recommend leaving them as they are.
10. **Older-data keywords (ⓞ).** 23 secondary placements and some primaries use October 12–13 data, mostly lamb rack, cutlet, mince and kofta terms that weren't in the refreshed files. To refresh them, search in Semrush: lamb rack, lamb cutlets, lamb mince, lamb kofta, lamb kebab, rack of lamb.

The same data is in `docs/keyword-report-2026-11-02.csv`, one row per keyword. It opens in Excel and can be filtered by item, role, intent or KD.
